### [FIXED] Concurrent alert acknowledgements produce duplicate audit rows and overwrite attribution, violating the documented "first ack wins" contract

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 19 — Alert-Ack Concurrency Race
- **Files changed**: `backend/app/services.py:456-501` (`acknowledge_alert` rewritten around a conditional UPDATE claim), `backend/tests/test_phase19_alert_ack_race.py` (new — 4 tests)
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced live this session with a scratch probe outside the repo (deleted afterwards): disposable Postgres database `wardress_p19_probe` built by `alembic upgrade head` in the pre-existing `wardress-test-pg` container, real app over ASGI transport, 4 barrier-synchronized `POST /api/alerts/{id}/ack` requests (alternating admin/analyst tokens) ×3 alerts → every racer returned 200 and each alert carried **4 `alert.acknowledge` audit rows** where the docstring promises exactly one; attribution was last-committer-wins. Matches the audit's own evidence ([4, 4, 4] across alerts) row for row. Not stale: no prior phase had touched `acknowledge_alert` (fix-log re-read + git log).
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: the ack guard is a plain read of the request's session-local ORM copy (`if alert.acknowledged_at is None`) followed by attribute writes and commit. Under READ COMMITTED, every concurrent transaction validates the precondition against its own pre-commit snapshot; Postgres never re-evaluates the application-level `if`, so all K writers proceed, each stages an audit row via `record_audit`, and the row-level UPDATEs serialize with last-committer-wins semantics for every column — N audit rows plus potentially wrong attribution (the investigator cannot tell who acked first). No constraint is violated because all writers write legal values; nothing arbitrates. The missing primitive is a conditional UPDATE whose WHERE predicate Postgres re-evaluates when a losing writer's lock wait ends (EvalPlanQual) — exactly the codebase's established atomic-claim primitive.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *K concurrent acks, same surface* (dashboard racers, admin+analyst mix) → all K return 200 (idempotent-success contract preserved by design — this endpoint has no 409 shape), exactly ONE winner records state + audit row, attribution = the first committer. Tested K=4 ×3 alerts through the real API.
  - *Cross-surface race* (dashboard vs Telegram bot — the docstring's named pair, including the bot's actor=None/actor_label shape) → one claim arbitrates; if the bot wins, `acknowledged_by` stays NULL and the audit row carries actor_label "telegram-bot"; coherent either way. Tested at service level with barrier-synchronized sessions.
  - *Sequential re-ack (pre-existing contract)* → unchanged: rowcount 0 path returns the row refreshed-but-unchanged, records nothing; pre-existing `test_acknowledge_alert_idempotent` stayed green unmodified.
  - *Response honesty* → sessions are `expire_on_commit=False`, so after a Core UPDATE the caller's object holds stale values; explicit `db.refresh(alert)` on BOTH paths (winner post-commit, loser post-miss) makes every responder reflect the one committed state. Verified empty deliveries relationship survives refresh (no lazy-load trap for `_detail`'s `alert.deliveries`; probed empirically before relying on it).
  - *Empty/null/malformed input*: N/A — UUID-typed path param; service receives a persisted instance; no new parsing surface. Unicode/boundary: N/A beyond unchanged `via` values ("dashboard"/"telegram"/agent surfaces, ≤32-col).
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: winner's ack + audit row still commit atomically (Phase 2's verified record_audit staging); a failed commit rolls back both loudly — fail-closed, never a silent double-audit.
  - *Retry/idempotency*: retried/re-delivered acks land in the rowcount-0 path — no duplicate effects.
  - *Auth/RBAC interaction*: none changed — AnalystUser router guard, agent tier-1 gating (Phase 11), and bot authorization all untouched; the fix only arbitrates which authorized acker wins.
  - *Alert deleted mid-race* (site/scan CASCADE between read and claim): rowcount 0 then refresh raises loudly — identical loud-failure class to pre-fix (whose blind ORM UPDATE would raise StaleDataError on 0 matched rows); not a new failure mode, window is micro-scale.
  - *Interaction with prior fixes (Rule 11)*: Phase 2 audit staging reused as-is; Phases 4/5/18 established the exact primitive and response-refresh idiom mirrored here (their comment style matched); Phase 11's injection gate wraps this service unchanged (its acknowledge tests re-ran green); Phase 17 unrelated. Agent tool + Telegram bot inherit the fix through the shared services layer with zero call-site changes.
  - *Backward compatibility*: no schema/model/enum/API-shape change; AlertOut fields unchanged; historical rows unaffected; frontend renders acknowledged_at/via as before.
  - *Performance*: one indexed conditional UPDATE replaces SELECT-guard+unit-of-work UPDATE; refresh adds one SELECT per ack — nil against HTTP/LLM costs.
  - *Fix-failure mode*: any unexpected error in the claim raises loudly before audit staging — fail-closed direction (worst case an error where pre-fix silently corrupted the trail).
- **Fix design considered (Step 4)**: (A) conditional UPDATE + rowcount arbiter + refresh-on-both-paths, audit staged only by the winner — **chosen**: DB-level arbitration, zero lock held across awaits, keeps the designed no-409 idempotent-success contract, and matches the codebase precedent (refresh rotation auth.py:280-292; remediation confirm/dismiss Phases 4/5; scan/baseline supersession Phase 18; login lockout extend-only UPDATE Phase 17). (B) `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` then mutate — rejected: holds the row lock across audit staging + commit and needs the async dance; rejected for the same reasons it was rejected in Phase 4. (C) Process-local asyncio lock — bypassed by multi-process workers/bot surfaces; rejected. (D) Partial unique index à la scans/baselines — not applicable: there is no in-flight status pair to constrain; ack is a single nullable-column transition, for which the conditional UPDATE is the right-sized primitive. The audit's suggested fix direction ("UPDATE … WHERE … AND acknowledged_at IS NULL, skip the audit row when 0 rows updated") is implemented verbatim.
- **Fix applied (Step 5)**: `acknowledge_alert` now claims `acknowledged_at IS NULL → acked` via a Core conditional UPDATE setting `acknowledged_at`/`acknowledged_by`/`acknowledged_via`; rowcount 0 → `db.refresh(alert)` + return unchanged (no audit, no commit); rowcount 1 → stage the `alert.acknowledge` audit row (unchanged payload shape incl. risk_score/via after-json), commit, refresh, return. Docstring extended to document the claim mechanism. No other production file touched.
- **Tests added/modified (Step 6)**: new `backend/tests/test_phase19_alert_ack_race.py` (4 tests, real app / real services against real Postgres, commit-window shim guarantees overlap while the DB arbitrates outcomes):
  - `test_concurrent_acks_single_winner_single_audit_row` — 4 racers ×3 alerts alternating admin/analyst tokens through the real API: all 200, exactly 1 audit row per alert, `acknowledged_by == audit.actor_id` (coherent attribution). **FAILED pre-fix** (4 audit rows per alert — the filed signature).
  - `test_every_response_reflects_the_one_committed_state` — all four race responses report the SAME acknowledged instant, equal to the committed DB value. **FAILED pre-fix** (each loser echoed its own doomed overwrite while another commit won).
  - `test_mixed_surface_race_bot_vs_dashboard_single_winner` — dashboard vs bot-shaped acks from two barrier-synchronized sessions: both callers observe ONE committed state; exactly 1 audit row; bot-win ⇒ NULL by + "telegram-bot" actor_email; dashboard-win ⇒ admin id + email. **FAILED pre-fix** (surfaces observed different states, 2 audit rows).
  - `test_reack_after_race_still_idempotent` — behavior-preservation guard passing both sides: late sequential acks change nothing, add nothing.
  - **Failing-before proof performed formally**: `git stash push -- backend/app/services.py` → module run against the pre-fix tree → **exactly 4 FAILED** with signatures quoted above; stash popped cleanly, all green re-verified post-pop.
- **Full regression result (Step 7)**: Backend `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short` → **729 passed, 0 failed, 1 warning** (~577 s; warning = the pre-existing apprise/imghdr DeprecationWarning; 725 prior + 4 new). Frontend: `pnpm test` → **7 files / 40 passed**; `pnpm type-check` (tsc -b --noEmit) → exit 0; `pnpm exec oxlint src` → 0 errors / 12 warnings (= recorded baseline exactly). Backend `uv run --frozen ruff check .` → All checks passed! (after binding the loop-variable closures B023 flagged into a shared `_race_acks` helper); `ruff format --check .` → 137 files formatted. Directly-neighboring suites re-run green after the format pass (`test_phase19 + test_services + test_agent_prompt_injection` → 32 passed). No dependency changes → supply-chain gates stand green from Phase 9. Live probe re-run post-fix: same race shape, all-200 preserved, exactly **1 audit row per alert**.
- **Interactions with prior fixes**: Phase 2 — record_audit's atomic-with-caller-commit staging consumed unchanged. Phases 4/5/18 — the same conditional-claim primitive applied once more; their subsystems untouched and their suites re-ran green inside the 729. Phase 11 — agent acknowledge gating wraps this service; its 23 containment tests re-ran green. Phase 1 — harness conventions followed (real-Postgres race tests, loud-fail). No earlier phase modified `acknowledge_alert` or `routers/alerts.py`'s ack endpoint (verified via fix-log re-read + git log).
- **Residual risk / follow-ups**: (1) Attribution under a race resolves to the first COMMITTER (lock-order arbitration), which may differ from the first REQUEST to arrive by milliseconds — this IS "first ack wins" at the only level a database can define it, and the audit log now names exactly who that was. (2) The docs do not document ack-race semantics anywhere (nothing to sync; doc sweeps are Phases 33/34/41 anyway). (3) New leads observed: none.
- **Commit**: fix(phase19) — atomic claim makes concurrent alert acks single-winner with coherent attribution

### [FIXED] Layer 8 semantic drift is blind past a 5,000-char cap, near-zero in the realistic cosine band, and English-only — non-English partial rewrites score 0.0 end-to-end

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 20 — Detection Layer 8 Weakness
- **Files changed**: `backend/worker/detection/semantics.py` (module docstring rewritten; `_AGGRESSION_LEXICON`/`_TOPIC_KEYWORDS` gain non-Latin campaign phrasings; new `_EMBED_CHUNK_CHARS`/`_MAX_CHUNKS_PER_SIDE`/`_DRIFT_ANCHORS` constants; new `_chunks`/`_direction_similarity`/`drift_from_similarity`/`_embedded_windows`; `layer8_semantics` drift section rewired to multi-window comparison + calibrated mapping; three additive diagnostic evidence keys), `backend/tests/test_phase20_semantics_drift.py` (new — 29 tests), `backend/tests/test_fusion_integration.py:198-203` (one fixture wiggle amended, all assertions unchanged)
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced on current code with scratch probes outside the repo (deleted afterwards), using a production-faithful stub (applies `embed_text`'s own `text[:5000]` truncation) AND genuine MiniLM from the local HF cache. Filed holes confirmed: ~17k spam chars appended beyond the cap → similarity exactly 1.0 / drift exactly 0.0 under BOTH embedders; partial-Arabic takeover late in a large Latin page → score 0.0 end-to-end (zero lexicon hits, sim 1.0); early small spam measured sim 0.7994 → drift 0.0595 (inert band). NEW evidence beyond the filed text: the layer is actually blind past ~1,250 characters, not 5,000 — spam placed at char ~250/500 measured sim 0.888 while spam at char ~1250/2500/5000 measured sim EXACTLY 1.0, and `embed(first 900 chars)` is cos-1.0-identical to `embed(an entire 40k page)` — the encoder's own ~256-token window makes the shipped effective coverage the first few percent of any real page. The finding understates the blindness; the fix targets the real window.
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: one shared mechanism with three expressions. (1) Each side's entire visible text was reduced to a SINGLE fixed-window embedding before comparison (`embed_text(text[:5_000])`, itself further truncated by the encoder's token window): any mutation confined beyond that window yields byte-identical vectors ⇒ cosine exactly 1.0 ⇒ drift 0 — structural blindness, not a threshold issue. (2) The linear mapping `(0.85 − sim)/0.85` reserved its range for sim ∈ [0, 0.85] while real benign/attack comparisons cluster at sim ≥ 0.75, so realistic-band evidence scored 0.02–0.06 — indistinguishable from noise. (3) Both keyword channels were English-only, so non-Latin defacement vocabulary produced no lexical signal, and the window blindness diluted what little whole-text embedding shift a non-Latin banner produced. Missing primitives: multi-region comparison with proportional aggregation, a monotone mapping anchored to measured similarity bands, and non-Latin lexical coverage.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *Concurrent access*: N/A — stateless pure function of stored artifacts per scan (same analysis as Phases 6/7); executed inside the existing detection thread.
  - *Empty/null/missing input*: blank-side guard retained verbatim (similarity skipped when either visible text is blank); `embed_text` never raises (its own contract, unchanged); windows are never empty (chunker only runs on non-blank text).
  - *Wrong-type/malformed*: per-pair dimension mismatch or degenerate zero-vectors are excluded via `cosine_similarity`'s None contract; all-windows-degraded degrades to similarity None / drift 0.0 / score 0.0 (existing suite pins this; parametrized again in the new file).
  - *Unicode/non-English*: first-class this phase — Arabic/Russian/Chinese campaign phrasings added to the graded lexicons (specific multi-word forms mirroring the English entries' FP posture, still matched on NEW text only), and chunk-local comparison lets genuine cross-language embedding distance register on small pages instead of being averaged away.
  - *Oversized input*: encode budget bounded at ≤ 2×24 calls per scan regardless of page size; beyond ~14.4k covered characters per side the same number of windows is spread EVENLY across the whole text (sampling, not full reading) so no region is ever invisible — pinned by a tail-marker test at 240k chars.
  - *Boundary numerics*: mapping anchors asserted exactly (both plateau edges 1.00/0.90, segment interiors, sub-floor clamp for negative cosines which real cross-lingual pairs produce); monotonicity sweep over [-0.59, 1.19]; identical-multichunk-text control asserts sim approx(1.0, abs 1e-9) AND drift EXACTLY 0.0 through the new path.
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: failed/degenerate single windows are excluded rather than fatal (tested: partial outage keeps valid-window signal; total outage degrades cleanly); layer-level crash isolation remains as last resort.
  - *Retry/idempotency*: deterministic pure function; acks_late redelivery recomputes identically.
  - *Auth/RBAC interaction*: N/A — worker-internal computation; no endpoint/guard touched.
  - *Interaction with other findings already fixed (Rule 11)*: Phase 12's real-vector suite re-ran GREEN UNMODIFIED (its loose bounds absorbed the mechanism change: the exact-zero identical control is preserved structurally by the plateau, the rewrite bounds hold); Phase 10's operating-point guards re-ran green after a fixture amendment (below); Phase 6's gate untouched (layer 8 remains hash-gated for identical serialized DOM — DOM-derived layers stay in `GATED_BY_IDENTICAL_HASH`); suppression strips subtrees before extraction (unchanged seam); `explain.py` and scan_tasks' escalation overwrite consume `semantic_similarity` (float|None) unchanged; dataset builder's imports (`_AGGRESSION`, `_TOPICS`, `embed_text`) keep their shapes so future regenerations pick up the extended lexicons automatically.
  - *Backward compatibility*: no schema/model/enum change; three additive diagnostic evidence keys (`semantic_chunks_baseline/current`, `semantic_min_chunk_similarity`) — frontend renders unknown keys not at all (verified Phase 10); persisted historical findings render as stored.
  - *Performance at realistic scale*: worst measured case 2.1 s for a 200k-char page pair (48 encodes) inside the detection thread; typical pages use 1–4 windows per side (~old cost). Bounded and documented.
  - *Fix-failure mode*: any unexpected exception lands in run_detection's per-layer crash isolation (skip_result with reason) — degraded-but-honest, never a crashed scan.
- **Fix design considered (Step 4)**:
  - (A) Chunked best-match containment with length-weighted SYMMETRIC mean + piecewise-linear calibrated anchors + lexicon extension — **chosen**: best-match pairing makes positional shifts invisible (prepend-banner guard test), length weighting keeps the metric proportional to affected content share (benign dynamic bits stay silent — measured 0.978–0.993 post-fix), symmetric min catches pure-append AND pure-deletion directions, and anchors make the full [0,1] drift scale reachable from realistic similarities.
  - (B) Aligned index-pair window comparison (window i vs window i) — rejected: any insertion/deletion shifts every subsequent pair, manufacturing drift from pure positional movement.
  - (C) min over all pairs / low-quantile aggregation — analyzed and rejected as the primary aggregate with worked examples: min is hostage to a single rotating-ad region (wholesale content swap in one region scores near-zero similarity regardless of page share — an FP channel the trained operating point cannot absorb before dataset regeneration); a low quantile provably MISSES small-append attacks entirely (a 1–2-window append into a 16-window page sits below the q25 order statistic and never moves it — quantiles ignore how few chunks are unmatched). min is retained as the recorded `semantic_min_chunk_similarity` diagnostic instead.
  - (D) Multilingual embedder swap — rejected for this phase: model/Dockerfile.worker/dataset-artifact churn far beyond the finding's scope; lexicon extension chosen (the audit's own "or" branch). Deviation recorded; multilingual embeddings remain the durable upgrade path alongside dataset regeneration.
  - (E) Steeper linear `(0.90−sim)/0.90` or sqrt/quadratic reshaping — rejected: the linear variant leaves the filed inert band essentially intact at 0.83 (0.078); nonlinear reshaping inflates the ≥0.95 noise floor (sim 0.95 → 0.13), colliding with the runtime benign-dynamic guards Phase 10 pinned.
  - Sub-decision during Step 6 iteration: the first anchor set used a 0.95 plateau and `zip(..., strict=True)`; testing exposed BOTH defects — strict-zip raised ValueError on negative cosines (masked by pipeline crash-isolation into a skip_result: exactly the vacuous-degradation class), and the 0.95 plateau let a single-sentence addition on a SMALL page measure sim 0.9309 → drift 0.038 → fused 0.376, breaking Phase 12's pinned "escalation never consulted for sub-band benign noise" contract. Fixed at root: explicit `strict=False` with an explicit fall-through clamp, and the plateau lowered to 0.90 (measured benign floor across all runtime fixtures: 0.93+). Neither patched around; both now pinned by tests.
- **Fix applied (Step 5)**: `semantics.py` — (1) `_chunks(text)`: windows spread across the WHOLE text (`n = min(24, ceil(len/600))`, evenly distributed starts; single window for short pages making the small-page path mathematically identical to the old compare modulo the mapping); (2) `_embedded_windows`: per-window `embed_text` (chunks sit far under the legacy cap, which stays in place for any direct caller); (3) `_direction_similarity`: length-weighted mean of each window's best-match cosine against all opposing windows + worst-window diagnostic, returning (None, None) when no valid pair exists; (4) aggregate = min(current-side mean, baseline-side mean) — proportional, insertion-immune, deletion-catching; (5) `drift_from_similarity`: piecewise-linear interpolation over descending anchors ((1.0→0),(0.90→0),(0.80→0.30),(0.50→0.90),(≤0→1.0)), documented against measured bands; (6) lexicons: Arabic تم اختراق / القراصنة, Russian сайт взломан / взломан / мы вернёмся, Chinese 被黑 / 已被入侵 aggression entries plus an Arabic contact-us topic pattern, weights mirroring their English tiers; (7) evidence gains the three additive diagnostics; module docstring states coverage bounds and sampling honestly.
- **Tests added/modified (Step 6)**: new `tests/test_phase20_semantics_drift.py` (29 tests). Stub discipline documented in-file: every fake embedder applies the production `_EMBED_CHAR_CAP` truncation BEFORE vectorizing — without it, uncapped stubs would detect beyond-cap mutations under the OLD code too, making the failing-before proofs vacuous. Coverage: 13-way exact anchor parametrize incl. negative-cosine clamp; full monotonicity sweep; plateau-exactness contract; THE headline regression (17k-char beyond-cap spam registers: sim < 0.85, drift ≥ 0.15, multi-window engaged — pre-fix measured exactly 1.0/0.0); tail-of-240k-page sampling reachability (min-chunk sees the mutation while the aggregate stays proportional); identical-multichunk exact-zero control; positional-shift silence guard; beyond-cap deletion detection; partial-outage exclusion semantics; total-degradation contract; encode-budget bound; Arabic/Russian/Chinese lexicon fires (parametrized); Arabic contact topic; baseline-present guard keeping the lexicon new-text-only; and the audit's own 17k-char SEO-spam row driven through the REAL task body end to end (flags, alert persisted, layer-8 evidence bounds asserted). **Failing-before proof performed formally two ways**: (a) `git stash push -- worker/detection/semantics.py` → committed module fails at collection (imports the new symbols); stash popped, 29 green re-verified; (b) scratch assertion runner against the stashed tree: beyond-cap spam FAIL (sim 1.0/drift 0.0), beyond-cap deletion FAIL (blind), Arabic lexicon FAIL (score 0.0, no hits), missing-mapping FAIL, identical-control PASS both sides — the designed split. One pre-existing test amended: `test_fusion_integration.py::test_visual_takeover_flags_end_to_end`'s fixture wiggle REPLACED `<h1>Acme</h1>` with a comment, deleting a word from a two-word page; the old inert mapping scored that self-inflicted semantic change ~0.05 (control fused 0.2236) while the calibrated mapping honestly measures it 0.29 (fused 0.84) — the wiggle now APPENDS a comment (visible text untouched, matching the Phase-12 file's established wiggle form), ALL original assertions retained unchanged (l4 > 0.3, control < 0.5, attack ≥ 0.5); amendment documented here per Rule 7/8 rather than silently accepted.
- **Full regression result (Step 7)**: Backend `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short` → **758 passed, 0 failed, 1 warning** (~558 s; warning = pre-existing apprise/imghdr DeprecationWarning; 729 prior + 29 new; includes the environment-sensitive DNS64 migration test passing). Frontend: `pnpm test` → **7 files / 40 passed**; `pnpm type-check` (tsc -b --noEmit) → exit 0; `pnpm exec oxlint src` → 0 errors / 12 warnings (= recorded baseline exactly). Backend `uv run --frozen ruff check .` → All checks passed!; `ruff format --check .` → 138 files formatted. No dependency changes → supply-chain gates stand green from Phase 9.
- **Interactions with prior fixes**: Phase 6 — gate membership untouched; its visual-gate suite green. Phases 7/9/10 — floors read only the fixed FEATURE_KEYS vector and compose max(model, floor): unaffected by emission changes; the fusion artifact/dataset are consumed read-only and remain byte-identical (their suites green inside the 758). Phase 12 — real-vector drift tests green UNMODIFIED (bounds held); escalation-band wiring exercised unchanged via the known-domain scenario. Phases 13–19 — no surface overlap; all suites green inside the regression. Phase 15's classifier/refit precedents followed where conventions applied (none needed this phase — no schema/deps touched).
- **Residual risk / follow-ups**: (1) Sub-proportional edits on very large pages dilute below the aggregate's noise floor BY DESIGN (proportionality is what keeps benign rotation quiet); the `semantic_min_chunk_similarity` diagnostic preserves per-region visibility for operators/tooling — if a verdict-level use for it emerges, that is a deliberate design change, not a bug fix. (2) Dense-script (CJK) pages: 600-char chunks still exceed the encoder's token window for ideographic text, so within-chunk truncation persists there — 24 sampled windows still cover vastly more than the old single ~900-char window; a script-aware chunk size is recorded as the regeneration-era refinement. (3) Distribution-shift hand-off STANDS and is now load-bearing: layer 8's emission profile changed, so the committed dataset/model (measured under the old profile) must be regenerated via the checked-in builder + trainer AFTER the emission-fix cluster completes (Phases 21–23/36), exactly per the workflow Phases 8/10 recorded. (4) Docs wording (`docs/layers/8-semantics.mdx`'s "full visible text" claim et al.) deliberately untouched — owned by the scheduled Phase 34/41 sweeps per §4.0.5; module docstring is truthful as of this commit. (5) New lead logged above: `_new_text`'s whole-sentence novelty granularity (shared layers 5/8 splitter) — observed live during fixture debugging, bounded impact, candidate for Phase 22/42.
- **Scratch hygiene**: probes lived in `%TEMP%\opencode\p20`, deleted after use; repo tree contains only intended changes plus the long-dispositioned items (`scripts/lib.ps1` user-intentional unstaged edit, untracked audit files).
- **Commit**: fix(phase20) — multi-window semantic comparison, calibrated drift mapping, non-Latin defacement lexicons

### [FIXED] Layer 7 cloaking soft knee admits up to ~50% divergent crawler-facing content at score 0.0

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 21 — Detection Layer 7 Weakness
- **Files changed**: `backend/worker/detection/cloaking.py` (module docstring rewritten; `_MIN_FOREIGN_TOKENS`/`_ADD_RAMP_LO`/`_REMOVE_RAMP_LO`/`_RAMP_HI` constants; new `_clamp01`/`_variant_cloak_score`; scoring section of `layer7_cloaking` rewired to a graded dual-channel model; additive per-variant diagnostics + top-level `scoring` evidence block), `backend/tests/test_phase21_cloaking_grade.py` (new — 24 tests), `backend/tests/test_detection_fusion_pipeline.py:158-176` (`test_layer7_cloaked_googlebot_detected` fixture enriched to a realistically-sized page, assertion unchanged)
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced on current code with a scratch probe outside the repo (deleted afterwards) driving the real `layer7_cloaking`. The audit's token-crafted sweep reproduced row for row: 500-token reference + additive spam 100/300/400/450/499 → similarity 0.833/0.625/0.556/0.526/0.501 → score **exactly 0.000 in every case**; 550 → 0.048, 700 → 0.167. The union-dilution generalization also confirmed: on a 2000-token reference even 600 foreign tokens (30% of the reference's vocabulary, crawler-only) scored 0.000. Not stale; no prior phase had touched `cloaking.py` (fix-log re-read + git log).
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: the score formula `(d − 0.5)/0.5 if d > 0.5 else 0.0` hard-zeros every pair below union-RELATIVE divergence 0.5. Union-relative divergence dilutes additive spam by the shared page vocabulary (`|A∪B|` grows with the untouched base), so an attacker can inject foreign content up to half the union at exactly zero score, and only ~58% divergence yields 0.167. Two structural absences compound it: nothing measures the addition relative to the REFERENCE (`|B∖A|/|A|`, which does not dilute), and nothing grades the intermediate band at all. The 0.5 knee itself existed for a real reason — token-set Jaccard exaggerates trivial edits on short pages ("Visitor #42" on a 3-token page measures union-divergence 0.4) — so a naive continuous ramp starting at 0.15–0.2 would have re-exposed that false-positive channel. The missing primitives are an absolute-mass churn grace separating dynamic bits from content campaigns, and a direction-aware graded curve over both divergence views.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *Concurrent access*: N/A — stateless pure function of stored probe artifacts per scan (same analysis as Phases 6/7/20); executed inside the existing detection thread.
  - *Empty/null/missing input*: both sides blank → similarity 1.0 preserved (union-size-0 guard); reference blank + variant has content, and the reverse, still saturate to 1.0 exactly as before (verified); missing variants / unusable reference / bot-blocking / fetch-error paths byte-identical (guards re-run green).
  - *Wrong-type/malformed*: set operations on split strings cannot raise; `layer_result` clamps defensively as before.
  - *Unicode/non-English*: whitespace tokenization unchanged (CJK granularity coarseness pre-existing, not worsened).
  - *Boundary numerics*: ramp anchors asserted parametrized — the grace edge is ABSOLUTE (exactly 12 foreign tokens graces on any page size; 13 grades, tested on a tiny page where the relative jump is largest); removal-ramp boundary kept off exact float-equality edges by construction margin.
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: layer-level crash isolation unchanged; any unexpected exception degrades to a skip result, never a crashed scan.
  - *Retry/idempotency*: deterministic pure function; acks_late redelivery recomputes identically.
  - *Auth/RBAC interaction*: N/A — worker-internal computation; no endpoint/guard touched.
  - *Interaction with prior fixes (Rule 11)*: Phase 7's `severe_cloaking` floor (trigger l7 ≥ 0.85 → floor 0.90) re-proven reachable end to end — total replacement saturates at 1.0 either direction, and a NEW pipeline test proves ADDITIVE cloaking on a hash-gated scan now reaches the floor too (pre-fix impossible: sub-knee scores could never arm it); Phase 10's constant-coherence guards green unmodified (floor values untouched); Phase 12's real-vector severe-cloaking e2e test passes UNMODIFIED (its fixture's removed-token mass exceeds the grace; removal-dominant path saturates via union divergence 1.0); Phase 6's gate membership untouched (layer 7 still runs under identical primary hash — regression-guarded). Dataset-builder interplay analyzed: its `benign_ua_variation` axis (aside removal, ~2–5 tokens) stays graced at 0.0, coherent with the builder's own "must stay below the knee" comment; its `cloaking_partial/heavy` attack axes will measure HIGHER after the recorded post-cluster regeneration (Phase 20's hand-off stands — artifacts deliberately NOT regenerated this phase).
  - *Deployed-model operating point (the phase's declared weighing)*: the refit artifact weights layer 7 heavily (+10.9420, intercept −2.2810) because training data only ever contained 0-or-~1 emissions under the old knee. Measured through the deployed composition post-fix: l7 = 0.0714 (audit's 100-spam row) fuses to **0.1825** — registers without alerting; l7 = 0.2143 fuses to 0.5160 (flag-threshold territory); l7 = 0.6429 (300-spam row) fuses to **0.9915 flagged**; full takeover ≈ 1.0 flagged. Substantial partial cloaking therefore FLAGS at defaults before any model regeneration — accepted deliberately: these are the scenarios the finding documents as silent misses, mitigations are the shipped ones (suppression rules run before fusion; executions are human-gated pending_confirm by default), and dataset/model regeneration after Phases 21–23/36 (recorded workflow from Phase 20) recalibrates honestly. Emission-side conservatism was placed where benign mass lives: every measured benign shape stays at exactly 0.0.
  - *False-positive danger analysis*: rotating ads/timestamps/counters produce single-digit unique token deltas (graced); aggressive widget rotation (~30 tokens on a 300+ token page) stays 0.0 because both channels sit below the 0.15 additive start; mobile-UA content stripping is routed to the conservative removal channel starting at 0.45 (≈ the historical knee) so responsive serving keeps today's silence; the residual FP surface is small-page large-relative-divergence (e.g. A/B swaps of 15+ distinct tokens on <100-visible-token pages) — rare in practice (real raw-HTML pages carry far more visible text) and documented here knowingly.
  - *Backward compatibility*: evidence keys consumed elsewhere preserved verbatim — per-variant `similarity` stays RAW Jaccard (`app/explain.py:111-120` keys on `< 0.8`), plus `worst_divergence` and `variants[].ua/http_status/comparable/error/note/identical_hash`; additions are per-variant `added_tokens/removed_tokens/reference_tokens/union_divergence/new_token_fraction/graced` and top-level `scoring` (frontend renders unknown keys not at all — verified Phase 10); persisted historical findings render as stored. No schema/model/config change.
  - *Performance*: two set differences plus arithmetic per comparable variant — nil against SSIM/embedding costs already paid.
  - *Fix-failure mode*: score clamped into [0,1] at both variant and layer level; monotone non-decreasing in added foreign mass (tested sweep); worst case equals old-style conservative silence, never an out-of-range emission.
- **Fix design considered (Step 4)**:
  - (A) Graded dual-channel curve + absolute churn grace + direction routing — **chosen**: closes the dead zone exactly where the audit's mechanism lives (additive spam diluted by the shared base), preserves the original knee's legitimate FP protection via the absolute grace instead of a relative cliff, and keeps UA-adaptive removal behavior conservative. Both of the audit's suggested mechanisms are implemented (continuous grading from a low start; reference-relative |B∖A|/|A|), with one documented deviation: the low start applies to the ADDITIVE-dominant channel while removal-dominant pairs keep a near-historical start, justified by the asymmetric threat shape (additive-to-crawler is the filed attack; removal overlaps benign mobile adaptation).
  - (B) Naive continuous ramp on union divergence alone (start 0.15–0.2) — rejected with measured cause: the shipped `mild_dynamic_variation` shape (2 added tokens on a 3-token page) measures union-divergence 0.40 → would emit ~0.29, breaking its exact-zero pin and manufacturing alerts from short-page churn — the exact FP channel the original knee existed to prevent.
  - (C) Symmetric removed-fraction channel or cross-variant quantile aggregation — rejected: removed-fraction punishes legitimate mobile adaptation; quantiles add nothing at ≤2 rotated UAs (worst-variant max retained as today).
  - (D) Character-n-gram/multilingual similarity upgrade — rejected: beyond this finding's scope (threshold calibration, not metric swap); token-set Jaccard retained as the similarity basis, consistent with Phase 20 fixing coverage mechanics without swapping the metric stack.
- **Fix applied (Step 5)**: `cloaking.py` — `_variant_cloak_score(ref_tokens, var_tokens)` computes added/removed/intersection/union, `union_divergence`, `new_token_fraction = min(1, added/max(1,|A|))`; pairs with `max(added, removed) <= _MIN_FOREIGN_TOKENS (12)` return 0.0 (`graced` flag in diagnostics); otherwise effective additive divergence `= max(union_divergence, new_token_fraction)` ramps [0.15 → 0.85], removal-dominant pairs (`added < removed`) use union divergence ramping [0.45 → 0.85], pure-additive uses the additive channel; `layer7_cloaking` aggregates the worst variant score (max), keeps hash-shortcut and degradation paths byte-identical, records compat evidence plus new diagnostics and a `scoring` anchor block. Module docstring states model, anchors, asymmetry rationale, and grace rationale honestly (docs-tree wording remains reserved for the scheduled Phase 34 sweep per §4.0.5).
- **Tests added/modified (Step 6)**: new `tests/test_phase21_cloaking_grade.py` (24 tests): audit-sweep rows no longer zero (parametrized 300/400/450/499, each ≥ 0.6); small-additive registers weakly (> 0); union-dilution on a 2000-token reference registers ≥ 0.2; ADDITIVE cloaking reaches the severe floor END TO END through `run_detection` on a hash-gated scan (l2 skipped, l7 ≥ 0.85, rule_floor fired — pre-fix unreachable since sub-knee scores could never arm the floor); monotonicity sweep over added mass 13→700; seven graced shapes pinned at EXACTLY 0.0 (timestamp tick, counter-on-tiny-page, ad-widget at the grace edge, 40% mobile strip, aside removal on a small page, blank-blank, blank-reference); absolute-grace boundary (12 vs 13 tokens on a tiny page); moderate reduction quiet; near-total hiding saturates ≥ 0.85; disjoint replacement saturates at 1.0; evidence contract (compat keys + new diagnostics + anchors block); similarity-stays-Jaccard guard; bot-blocking/error paths never score; identical-hash shortcut intact; layer-7-still-runs-under-identical-hash regression guard. **Failing-before proof performed formally**: `git stash push -- worker/detection/cloaking.py` → the committed module fails at collection pre-fix (imports the new symbols), AND a scratch behavioral runner against the stashed tree measured the three headline scenarios (499-spam, 300-spam, 600/2000 dilution) at exactly **0.0000** each — the filed signature; stash popped cleanly, all 24 green re-verified. One pre-existing test amended: `test_layer7_cloaked_googlebot_detected`'s fixture was a 3-visible-token page vs a 5-token spam page — absolute mass inside the churn grace, where 0.0 is the honest verdict for micro-pages; enriched to a realistic multi-sentence corporate page (assertion ≥ 0.7 unchanged), adaptation documented inline (record-keeping entry below).
- **Full regression result (Step 7)**: Backend `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short` → **782 passed, 0 failed, 1 warning** (~598 s; warning = pre-existing apprise/imghdr DeprecationWarning; 758 prior + 24 new; includes the environment-sensitive DNS64 migration test passing). Frontend: `pnpm test` → **7 files / 40 passed**; `pnpm type-check` (tsc -b --noEmit) → exit 0; `pnpm exec oxlint src` → 0 errors / 12 warnings (= recorded baseline exactly). Backend `uv run --frozen ruff check .` → All checks passed!; `ruff format --check .` → 139 files formatted (new files formatted before commit). No dependency changes → supply-chain gates stand green from Phase 9. Post-fix live probes quoted under the operating-point bullet above.
- **Interactions with prior fixes**: Phase 7 — floors untouched; their reachability EXPANDED (additive cloaking can now arm `severe_cloaking`, proven by the new pipeline-level test) while their no-fire guarantees hold (benign profiles score 0.0 at the source, so floors see nothing). Phase 12 — its severe-cloaking e2e scenario passes unmodified (removal-dominant saturation); its known-domain/benign-dynamic pins unaffected (layer-7 emissions for those fixtures remain 0.0). Phases 8–9 — artifacts consumed read-only, byte-identical; regeneration hand-off recorded below. Phase 6 — gate membership and crash-isolation semantics untouched (regression-guarded). No earlier phase modified `cloaking.py` (verified via fix-log re-read + git log).
- **Residual risk / follow-ups**: (1) Distribution-shift hand-off STANDS and now covers layer 7: the committed dataset/model were measured under the old knee; regenerate via the checked-in builder + trainer AFTER the emission-fix cluster completes (Phases 21–23/36), per the workflow Phases 8/10/20 recorded. Until then substantial partial cloaking flags at defaults by design (operating point weighed above). (2) Small-page large-relative-divergence shapes (A/B hero swaps on <100-token pages) ride mid-band rather than staying silent — accepted with cause; revisit only if operators report noise (the durable answer would be richer features, not threshold retuning). (3) CJK tokenization coarseness in `_text_similarity` is pre-existing and unchanged — whitespace splitting treats an ideographic sentence as few giant tokens; candidate alongside Phase 20's script-aware chunking note if crawler-vs-browser CJK cloaking ever needs finer grain. (4) Docs wording (`docs/layers/7-cloaking.mdx`'s knee description et al.) deliberately untouched — owned by the scheduled Phase 34 sweep; the module docstring IS truthful as of this commit. (5) New leads observed: none.
- **Scratch hygiene**: probes lived in `%TEMP%\opencode\p21`, deleted after use; repo tree contains only intended changes plus the long-dispositioned items (`scripts/lib.ps1` user-intentional unstaged edit, untracked audit files).
- **Commit**: fix(phase21) — graded dual-channel cloaking scoring with churn grace

### Pre-existing-test adaptation forced by this phase (record-keeping entry, not a finding fix)

- **Phase**: 21 — transparency per Rule 8
- **What changed**: `backend/tests/test_detection_fusion_pipeline.py::test_layer7_cloaked_googlebot_detected` built its "entirely different page" fixture from a page with only THREE visible tokens ("Acme Reliable widgets.") vs a five-token spam stub. Under the graded model that pair differs by 5 unique tokens — inside the churn grace — and correctly scores 0.0: a micro-page cannot support a statistical claim of "different site", which is precisely why the old relative-only knee exaggerated such edits. The test's intent (total replacement detected) is preserved by enriching BOTH fixture pages to realistic sizes (multi-sentence corporate page vs disjoint spam page, RICH_HTML constant added); the ≥ 0.7 assertion is unchanged and now expresses the property on data that can honestly carry it. Strengthen-the-fixture precedent: Phase 17's long-site adaptation of its own hook-label test.

### [FIXED] Layer 5 misses common leetspeak variants of its strongest signatures, and script-flip requires ≥60% dominance plus samples only the first 20k chars

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 22 — Detection Layer 5 Weakness
- **Files changed**: `backend/worker/detection/signatures.py` (module docstring rewritten; `_LEET_TRANSLATION` decode table; inflow gate constants; `_script_of_char` memoized via `functools.cache`; `Counter`-based `_script_counts` helper; `script_profile` defaults to FULL text with the cap demoted to an opt-in parameter; new `_collect_dual_view_hits` matcher; `layer5_signatures` rewired to dual-view matching + inflow rule + four additive evidence keys), `backend/tests/test_phase22_signatures_coverage.py` (new — 27 tests)
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced on current code with scratch probes outside the repo (deleted afterwards), driving the real `layer5_signatures`. (a) Leet: `H@CK3D BY XYZ CREW`, `PWN3D BY XYZ CREW`, `0WNED BY XYZ CREW`, `0WN3D BY XYZ CREW`, `D3FAC3D BY XYZ CREW`, lowercase `h@ck3d by` all scored **exactly 0.0000** while controls `HACKED BY`/`H4CK3D BY` scored 1.0000 — the audit's miss list verbatim. (b) Script flip: a ~49-char Arabic defacement banner injected into a Latin page measured c_profile `LATIN 0.988 / ARABIC 0.012` → no dominance flip, zero signature hits, score 0.0 end-to-end. (c) Cap: profiling a page whose first >20k chars are Latin with Arabic appended after char 20,000 returned `{LATIN: 1.0}` under the shipped default — tail invisible. One nuance beyond the filed text: for tails LARGE relative to the first 20k window, truncation still admitted the tail (it dominates the truncated window); the structurally-blind shape is same-script-prefix pages, which is what the fix's test pins. Not stale: `git log -- worker/detection/signatures.py` shows the file untouched since its original commit — no prior phase had modified it.
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: three independent coverage holes in one layer. (1) Signature matching enumerated literal spellings (`\bh4ck3d\b`, `\bhack3d\b`) instead of decoding the substitution algebra, so any un-enumerated leet form of even the strongest phrases scored zero — and tier-1 phrases are precisely what the Phase-7 conclusive floor keys on, so each missed spelling also disarms that downstream bound. (2) The script-mixing signal required BOTH sides to hold a ≥0.6 dominant script AND the dominant bucket to change — a whole-page-rewrite detector — leaving the partial-injection class (foreign banner into a majority-Latin page) with no representation at all. (3) `script_profile(text[:20_000])` made script content past the sample line count-equivalent to nonexistent for both flip inputs. Missing primitives: algebra-level matching at the boundary, an inflow-shaped script signal with false-positive gates, and unbounded-but-cheap script counting.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *Concurrent access*: N/A — stateless pure function of stored artifacts per scan (same analysis as Phases 6/7/20/21); executed inside the existing detection thread.
  - *Empty/null/missing input*: blank-side guards preserved verbatim (`script_profile("")=={}`; identical-rescan still 0.0 — pinned); `_collect_dual_view_hits` over empty text yields nothing.
  - *Wrong-type/malformed*: undecodable-bytes page fixture re-run green (existing suite + layer contract unchanged); per-character classification never raises (unicodedata ValueError → None bucket).
  - *Unicode/non-English*: first-class this phase — Arabic/Cyrillic/CJK banners parametrized as inflow triggers; kana+CJK aggregate correctly because buckets sum (`_script_counts` counts per bucket, inflow sums added buckets).
  - *Boundary numerics*: absolute grace asserted at exactly 39 (quiet on ANY page size) / 40 (fires where the page-share floor is also met); `_NEW_SCRIPT_BASELINE_EPSILON` 0.01 and `_NEW_SCRIPT_PAGE_SHARE` 0.05 exercised via fixture arithmetic; baseline floor blocks inflow on near-empty baselines (dominance flip covers stub-page takeovers instead — pinned).
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: unexpected exceptions land in run_detection's per-layer crash isolation exactly as before; scores clamp through `layer_result`.
  - *Retry/idempotency*: deterministic pure function; acks_late redelivery recomputes identically; `functools.cache` on `_script_of_char` is process-lifetime sound (Unicode DB immutable per interpreter).
  - *Auth/RBAC interaction*: N/A — worker-internal computation; no endpoint/guard touched.
  - *Interaction with other findings already fixed (Rule 11)*: **Phase 7** — leet variants now reach signature_score 1.0, arming `conclusive_signature_text` (trigger l5 ≥ 0.85 → floor 0.90): its floor tests re-ran green and its reachability EXPANDED exactly along the filed miss-list. **Phase 11** — injection containment relies on evidence `matched` strings being verbatim page text; the dual-view matcher slices evidence from the RAW text at decoded-view spans (1:1 translate ⇒ identical positions), so fencing/explain behavior is unchanged (pinned by a dedicated verbatim-evidence test). **Phase 12** — its e2e pins re-ran green UNMODIFIED. **Suppression** — rules strip subtrees BEFORE extraction, so suppressed injections still cannot fire anything (existing suite green). **Dataset regeneration hand-off STANDS**: `sig_leet` rows and cross-language attack axes will measure HIGHER after this change; regeneration happens after the emission-fix cluster (Phases 21–23/36) per the workflow recorded in Phases 8/10/20/21 — deliberately NOT regenerated here.
  - *Backward compatibility*: no schema/model/config change; four ADDITIVE evidence keys (`scripts_added`, `new_script_chars`, `new_script_page_share`, boolean `script_inflow`); every pre-existing key preserved byte-compatible (`script_flip` still means dominance-only — explain.py's wording stays true); persisted historical findings render as stored; frontend renders unknown keys not at all (verified Phase 10).
  - *Performance at realistic scale*: full-text profiling made cheaper than the old capped loop at realistic sizes by counting over `Counter(text)` (C-speed) so only unique characters pay classification: naive per-char full-text = 1.007 s on a 1.47M-char text vs memoized unique-char counting ≈ tens of ms; typical ≤20k pages are single-digit ms inside the detection thread; `str.translate` decode cost measured 0.02 ms per 200 KB — nil against SSIM/MiniLM costs already paid.
  - *Fix-failure mode*: misclassification worst case equals old conservative silence or a clamped 0..1 emission — fail-safe direction; the matcher raises nothing new (pure regex/set operations).
- **Fix design considered (Step 4)**:
  - *Leet*: (A) length-preserving decode table (@→a, 4→a, 3→e, 0→o, 1→i, 5/$→s, 7→t) used as a SECOND matching view with span-aware dedup against raw — **chosen**: covers arbitrary mixed forms ("H@CK3D", "0WN3D") at the algebra level, keeps evidence verbatim, zero pattern-list churn. (B) enumerating more variant literals — rejected: combinatorial whack-a-mole that provably misses mixes. (C) unicode NFKC/casefold only — rejected: digit/symbol substitutions untouched.
    - Sub-decision forced by testing: naive dual-corpus matching double-counts exact-substitution terms through redundant patterns — live-measured: "GR33TZ" summed `\bgr33tz\b` (raw view) + `\bgreetz\b` (decoded view) to weight 1.1 → score 1.0, arming the CONCLUSIVE fusion floor on a medium-tier term. Fixed at root in `_collect_dual_view_hits`: a decoded hit is dropped only when a RAW hit at the SAME SPAN decodes to identical text (the same term through two views), while overlapping-but-distinct matches (standalone `h4ck3d` + the longer decoded `hacked by` phrase) remain separate exactly as unrelated patterns would be. Both behaviors pinned by tests.
  - *Inflow*: (A) tri-gate inflow signal (baseline-established floor 40 alpha chars ∧ absolute foreign-mass grace ≥40 chars ∧ foreign share of the WHOLE current page ≥0.05) feeding the existing 0.7 slot beside the dominance flip — **chosen**: mirrors Phase 21's grace lesson (absolute mass separates dynamic bits from campaigns), keeps the operating point legible, additive diagnostics. (B) lowering dominance thresholds — rejected: FPs on balanced bilingual pages globally. (C) graded inflow weight — rejected: continuous tuning unverifiable until dataset regeneration; binary-with-gates composes predictably with the deployed model (l5=0.7 fuses ≈0.999 — flagged, matching the finding's demand). (D) denominator choice — DEVIATION from the audit's literal "N% of new text", with live-measured cause: the sentence-piece splitter glues unpunctuated injections together with neighboring Latin fragments, so a PURE banner injection measured only 0.451 foreign share-of-new-text — the audit's own headline scenario would have stayed silent under that gate; the page-share floor (0.05 of current-page alphabetics) is immune to splitter glue while still rejecting incidental fragments.
  - *Cap*: (A) default to full text with memoized unique-char classification — **chosen** (perf above; cap retained as explicit opt-in parameter, API-compatible). (B) chunked profiles fused like layer 8 — rejected: chunking solves vector-window blindness, not counting; unnecessary complexity. (C) raise to a bigger fixed cliff — rejected: same structural flaw, later.
- **Fix applied (Step 5)**: `signatures.py` — docstring states both matching views, the dedup rule, and both script sub-rules honestly; `_LEET_TRANSLATION` length-preserving table; `_BASELINE_ALPHA_FLOOR=40/_NEW_SCRIPT_MIN_CHARS=40/_NEW_SCRIPT_BASELINE_EPSILON=0.01/_NEW_SCRIPT_PAGE_SHARE=0.05/_SCRIPT_SIGNAL_SCORE=0.7` documented constants; `@cache _script_of_char`; `_script_counts` over `Counter`; `script_profile(text, sample_cap=None)` full-text default; `_collect_dual_view_hits(patterns, raw, decoded)` implementing the content-aware cross-view dedup (evidence always sliced from raw); `layer5_signatures` runs signature+profanity loops over the union, computes dominance flip unchanged, adds the inflow rule, sets `flip_score` from either script signal, records the four new diagnostic keys. No other production file touched.
- **Tests added/modified (Step 6)**: new `tests/test_phase22_signatures_coverage.py`, 27 tests. Coverage: the six filed leet spellings parametrized to score exactly 1.0; verbatim-evidence pinning (`matched == "H@CK3D BY"` quoted from raw, never the decoded spelling); `$h1t` profanity registration; GR33TZ non-double-count (exact 0.55 — the guard that caught the design defect during Step-6 iteration); H4CK3D-BY union keeping BOTH views' patterns at 1.0; baseline-leet rescan never flags (invariant preserved under the new matcher); partial Arabic/Cyrillic/CJK injections firing inflow at 0.7 with dominance-flip False; grace boundary 39-vs-40; stray-fragment quiet; tiny-fragment-on-large-page quiet (page-share gate); unestablished-baseline block with dominance flip legitimately firing instead; dominance path byte-unchanged; benign dynamic edits stay 0.0; full-text profile past the old cap (default args) with explicit-cap API compat retained; layer-level beyond-cap visibility; evidence key shapes incl. zeroed diagnostics on clean edits; THE end-to-end proof — partial Arabic takeover flags through real `run_detection` + deployed refit model (audit measured fused 0.041 with every layer 0.0 for this class; now l5=0.7 → flagged ≥0.5); clean-rescan pipeline control. **Failing-before proof performed formally**: `git stash push -- worker/detection/signatures.py` → module run against the stashed tree → **exactly 20 FAILED / 7 PASSED** — every fix-dependent test failing with the filed signatures (leet scores 0.0, empty match lists, `KeyError` on the new evidence keys, profile `{LATIN: 1.0}` past-cap, e2e 0.0 vs 0.7) while the 7 behavior-preservation guards passed both sides; stash popped cleanly, all 27 green re-verified.
- **Full regression result (Step 7)**: Backend `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short` → **809 passed, 0 failed, 1 warning**, run TWICE — once mid-phase (~589 s) and again on the exact final formatted tree (~606 s) after ruff's mechanical fixes; warning = the pre-existing apprise/imghdr DeprecationWarning recorded since Phase 0; includes the DNS64-sensitive migration test passing. Frontend: `pnpm test` → **7 files / 40 passed**; `pnpm type-check` (tsc -b --noEmit) → exit 0; `pnpm exec oxlint src` → 0 errors / 12 warnings (= recorded baseline exactly). Backend `uv run --frozen ruff check .` → All checks passed!; `ruff format --check .` → 140 files formatted. Directly-neighboring detection suites re-run green after formatting (145 tests across phases 22/layers/suppression/floors/fusion-integration/e2e-flagging/scan-tasks). No dependency changes → supply-chain gates stand green from Phase 9.
- **Interactions with prior fixes**: Phase 7 — floors untouched; their trigger surface expanded along exactly the filed miss-list (leet strong hits now reach 1.0 and arm the conclusive floor); all 26 floor tests green. Phase 11 — verbatim-quote contract preserved by construction and pinned; its 23 containment tests green inside the regression. Phases 6/20/21 — gate membership untouched (layer 5 remains hash-gated), shared-splitter semantics untouched, their suites green; amended fixtures unaffected. Phases 8–10 — artifacts consumed read-only, byte-identical; regeneration hand-off extended (see residual). Phase 12 — pins green unmodified. Phase 1 harness conventions followed (hermetic pure-function tests, loud-fail).
- **Residual risk / follow-ups**: (1) A legitimate large foreign-language content addition (≥40 chars, ≥5% of page) fires by design — the same knowingly-accepted tradeoff shape as Phase 21's partial-cloaking operating point; mitigations are the shipped ones (per-site suppression rules run before extraction; remediations human-gated by default; LLM escalation second opinion). (2) Foreign appendices below 5% of a large page stay silent (page-share floor) — those are pixel-visible cases owned by the visual channel post-Phase-6. (3) `explain.py` does not narrate the new inflow evidence (its script_flip sentence remains true as written); runtime UX/docs prose belongs to the scheduled sweeps per §4.0.5 — module docstring IS truthful as of this commit. (4) The Phase-20 lead on `_new_text`'s whole-sentence novelty granularity was weighed during this phase's denominator analysis (splitter-glue measurement above) and deliberately left unchanged — its own recorded condition (operators reporting noise) has not occurred; impact bounded by pattern-match requirements. (5) Dataset/model regeneration after the emission-fix cluster completes must include this layer's changed emissions (recorded workflow; builder imports the untouched pattern lists, so sampling continues to track them mechanically). (6) New leads observed: none.

### [FIXED] Layer 2 hidden-element counting only recognizes inline styles — class-stylesheet hiding, opacity:0, font-size:0, and offscreen positioning are invisible

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 23 — Detection Layer 2 Weakness
- **Files changed**: `backend/worker/detection/dom.py` (module docstring rewritten; `_is_hidden`/`_HIDDEN_STYLE_MARKERS` replaced by a conservative stylesheet resolver — `_parse_declarations`, `_selector_subjects`, `_stylesheet_rules`, `_HiddenContext`, `_decisive_hidden_property`; `_tree_stats` consumes the context and records per-reason attribution; `layer2_dom_structure` evidence gains one additive block), `backend/tests/test_phase23_dom_hidden.py` (new — 35 tests)
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced on current code with scratch probes outside the repo (deleted afterwards), driving the real `layer2_dom_structure` and real `run_detection`. The audit's sweep reproduced row for row: 30 hidden spam links via inline `display:none` → `hidden_counted=30/30`, layer2 = 1.0; the same 30 links hidden via a `.row{display:none}` stylesheet class, inline `opacity:0`, inline `font-size:0`, or offscreen positioning (`position:absolute;left:-9999px`) → `hidden_counted=0/30` in every case (layer2 stuck at churn-only 0.6). Minimal-payload contrast: ONE hidden spam paragraph scored layer2 **0.5034** inline vs **0.0857** for every non-inline variant of the identical payload. New end-to-end quantification on a realistic ~324-element page (where layer 8's proportional aggregate stays quiet and link farms reuse known domains/vocabulary so layers 3/5/8 see nothing): a 12-row same-domain hidden link farm scored l2 0.5034 / fused 0.4174 inline vs l2 ≈0.15 / fused ≈0.28 for the non-inline variants — hiding technique is the ONLY variable. Not stale; `git log -- worker/detection/dom.py` shows no fix-phase had touched it (last change: Phase 2 formatting).
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: `_is_hidden` inspected exactly two per-element channels — the `hidden` attribute and substring markers against the element's own `style` attribute (`display:none`/`visibility:hidden` literals). CSS hiding reaches elements through indirection the code never resolved: (1) rules in `<style>` blocks selecting by class/id/tag — the idiomatic way to write hiding — and (2) inline properties that hide without display/visibility (`opacity:0`, `font-size:0`, negative absolute/fixed offsets, negative text-indent, large negative translate). The missing primitive is an element→"effectively hidden" resolution computed against the page's own embedded styling context.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *Concurrent access*: N/A — stateless pure function of stored artifacts per scan (same analysis as Phases 6/7/20/21/22); executed inside the existing detection thread.
  - *Empty/null/missing input*: pages with no `<style>` blocks degrade to attribute + inline-declaration detection (the old behavior, with strictly better normalization); blank-side guards in layer2 unchanged.
  - *Wrong-type/malformed CSS*: comment stripping + brace-aware tolerant parsing never raise; garbage/unclosed braces/unterminated comments/pseudo selectors yield no rules and inline detection still works (pinned); `_stylesheet_rules` wraps its whole body in try/except returning no rules as last resort.
  - *Unicode/CSS normalization*: uppercase property names/values and arbitrary whitespace handled (`DISPLAY : NONE` counted — pre-fix the substring check MISSED this too, so normalization is an additional repair); `!important` stripped; unparsable values contribute nothing.
  - *Selector subset (documented limitations)*: subject = LAST simple compound of a descendant sequence (`.side .ad` matches every `.ad` — over-match accepted, still requires class+declaration coincidence); pseudo-classes/pseudo-elements and attribute selectors skipped entirely (conditional applicability); @-blocks (@media/@supports/@keyframes/@font-face…) skipped WHOLE so print/responsive stylesheets can never manufacture hidden counts — bare `@media screen` hiding stays unseen (rare spam vector; churn/l8 still see the mass; documented residual).
  - *Cascade approximation*: document-order last-wins per property among matching rules; inline style overrides all sheet declarations (true per CSS spec for non-!important). A late generic element reset defeating an earlier class hide is the known mis-approximation direction (FN, conservative).
  - *Offscreen thresholds*: any negative offset under `position:absolute|fixed`; `text-indent ≤ −100px`; `translate*(…)px` component `≤ −500px`. Percentage translates are NEVER counted (carousel slides sit at ±100% legitimately — pinned); moderate sub-threshold negatives stay silent (boundary tests from imported constants).
  - *Non-rendering elements excluded*: html/head/body/meta/link/title/base/script/style/template/noscript tags plus `input[type=hidden]` (CSRF tokens must not count — pinned). Per-element counting (not subtree inflation), consistent with existing inline semantics; visibility inheritance not chased (consistent with pre-fix behavior).
  - *Boundary numerics*: thresholds asserted from just-below and at-the-edge fixtures built off the imported constants; opacity `0`/`0.0`; font-size `0`/`0px`.
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: unexpected exceptions land in run_detection's per-layer crash isolation exactly as before; resolver failure degrades to attribute+inline detection — never worse than pre-fix (fail-safe direction).
  - *Retry/idempotency*: deterministic pure function; acks_late redelivery recomputes identically.
  - *Auth/RBAC interaction*: N/A — worker-internal computation; no endpoint/guard touched.
  - *Interaction with other findings already fixed (Rule 11)*: **Phase 6** — layer 2 remains in `GATED_BY_IDENTICAL_HASH` (its inputs ARE the hashed DOM string; regression-guarded test kept green). **Phase 7** — floors read only the FEATURE_KEYS vector; layer 2 deliberately carries NO floor ("layer 2 IS the laundering lever") so emission changes compose cleanly; all 26 floor tests green. **Phase 12** — its e2e hidden-spam fixture uses inline `display:none` and passes UNMODIFIED. **Suppression** — css_selector rules strip subtrees BEFORE extraction, so suppressed hidden farms still count zero (pinned end-to-end). **explain.py** — consumes `hidden_count` {baseline,current} dict shape, preserved byte-compatibly; narration now automatically reports non-inline hiding too. **Phases 8–10 artifacts** — consumed read-only; the dataset builder's own `hidden_spam_stealth` axis (build_fusion_dataset.py:711-725) already generates exactly this finding's four techniques, so the recorded regeneration workflow re-measures those rows correctly after this change (hand-off STANDS, deliberately not regenerated here).
  - *False-positive danger analysis (the core constraint)*: framework UI toggles (menus/tabs/carousels) hide via classes at a similar base rate to inline toggles the product already accepts; delta semantics protect steady-state pages (baseline carries its own UI-hidden elements — pinned by an sr-only parity guard); a single spurious new-hidden yields l2=0.5034 → fused ≈0.41, sub-threshold alone (measured); mitigations remain the shipped stack (suppression rules, human-gated remediations, LLM second opinion). The @-block skip and non-rendering exclusions remove the largest legitimate-hiding classes (print sheets, responsive breakpoints, metadata, CSRF inputs) up front.
  - *Backward compatibility*: scores change ONLY where computed-hidden elements exist — precisely the filed defect. One additive evidence block (`hidden_detection`: per-side `stylesheet_chars` + per-reason `hidden_by` attribution); every pre-existing key preserved; frontend renders unknown keys not at all (verified Phase 10); persisted historical findings render as stored. No schema/model/config change.
  - *Performance at realistic scale*: O(style-text length + element count) per side with C-speed regex/dict operations and precomputed class/id/tag indexes; style text capped at 1 MB; typical corporate page (<20 KB embedded CSS, few hundred elements) adds single-digit ms inside the detection thread — nil against SSIM/MiniLM costs already paid.
  - *Fix-failure mode*: any resolver error degrades to attribute-only counting with the layer completing normally — worst case equals pre-fix behavior, never out-of-range emissions (clamped by `layer_result` as before).
- **Fix design considered (Step 4)**: (A) self-contained conservative CSS-subset resolver over the page's own `<style>` blocks — **chosen**: zero new dependencies, operates on the captured DOM string we already hash/store, bounded complexity, implements both halves of the audit's suggested direction (class/id resolution against same-DOM style blocks; extended inline markers) with rule-level audit trail. (B) Full CSS engine (tinycss2/cssselect2) computing true cascade/specificity — rejected: two new dependencies (supply-chain + Dockerfile.worker churn) yet STILL blind to external stylesheet bytes (not part of `PageData.html`), so the marginal correctness over (A) is small while the blast radius is large. (C) Headless computed-style capture at fetch time (store per-element visibility in PageData) — rejected: changes the capture contract/schema end-to-end, far beyond a detection-layer scoring fix (feature-class change, Rule 2). Deviation from the audit's "ideally the fetched stylesheet artifacts": external stylesheet bytes are not captured anywhere in PageData; adding them would be new capture machinery — recorded rather than done.
- **Fix applied (Step 5)**: `dom.py` — `_HiddenContext(root)` collects all `<style>` text (capped), parses top-level rules (comments stripped; @-blocks skipped via depth-matched brace walk), extracts compound selector subjects, and indexes them by tag/class/id (+wildcard); `hidden_reason(el)` excludes non-rendering tags and `input[type=hidden]`, checks the `hidden` attribute, then folds matching rules' declarations in document order with the inline style last, and asks `_decisive_hidden_property` whether display/visibility/opacity/font-size/position-offsets/text-indent/transform hide the element — returning the DECIDING source's label ('hidden-attribute' | 'inline-style' | rule subject like '.row') for the audit trail. `_tree_stats` builds one context per side and counts via it, recording per-reason attribution; `layer2_dom_structure` emits the additive `hidden_detection` evidence block. `_is_hidden`/`_HIDDEN_STYLE_MARKERS` removed (superseded; grep confirmed no external callers). Module docstring states the dual-channel model and scope honestly.
- **Tests added/modified (Step 6)**: new `tests/test_phase23_dom_hidden.py`, 35 tests. Coverage: headline technique-independence (inline wrapper / per-row stylesheet / opacity / offscreen farm variants each counted ≥1, score >0.4; per-row class variant counts exactly 30 → score ≥0.99); equal-count technique-neutrality through the REAL pipeline (12 rows class-hidden vs 12 rows inline-hidden → identical l2 regime within 0.05 — the audit's "technique is the only variable" property); eight non-display inline styles parametrized (opacity 0/0.0, font-size 0/0px, absolute/fixed offsets, text-indent −9999px, translate −9999px); visibility hidden+collapse; carousel percentage-translate NOT counted; boundary guards below both magnitude constants; id/compound/multi-class/descendant-subject selectors; pseudo-class+attribute selectors skipped; @media print skipped whole; comments do not hide; cascade last-wins unhides; inline overrides sheet; case/whitespace variants (inline AND sheet — the former was broken even pre-fix); non-rendering/input-hidden exclusions; sr-only baseline-parity produces delta 0; malformed-CSS fail-safety ×4 shapes with inline still detected; evidence contract (preserved `hidden_count` shape + additive `hidden_by` per-source attribution incl. 'hidden-attribute'/'inline-style'/'.row'); unit contracts of the parse helpers incl. direct `_HiddenContext` use; suppression interplay end-to-end (css_selector removes the hidden farm before extraction → delta 0, `suppression_applied` present). **Failing-before proof performed formally**: `git stash push -- backend/worker/detection/dom.py` → the committed module fails at COLLECTION on the stashed tree (`_HiddenContext` absent), AND a symbol-free behavioral runner measured the filed signature there — inline 30/30 OK; stylesheet-class/opacity/font-size/offscreen deltas all 0 (**4 FAILURES**, exactly the defect); stash popped cleanly, all 35 green re-verified. Two Step-6 iterations were forced by testing (recorded per Rule 8): (a) the first resolver matched rules on ANY index hit, so `div.a.b{display:none}` leaked onto plain divs (31 counted where 2 were honest) — fixed at root with full compound-subject filtering (`_subject_matches`) after the cheap pre-filter, now pinned by `test_compound_and_multi_class_selectors`; (b) the first neutrality tests compared a 1-element inline wrapper against 12 class-counted rows — an unfair premise (per-element semantics differ by construction); rewritten to equal-count comparisons with the wrapper's weaker-but-detected behavior pinned explicitly.
- **Full regression result (Step 7)**: Backend `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short` → first run **843 passed, 1 failed, 1 warning** (~587 s): the failure was `tests/test_uninstall_backup_safety.py::test_full_backup_completes_and_teardown_proceeds` dying with `Unrecoverable error in PowerShell` (process returncode 2148734499) — a pwsh process CRASH, not an assertion failure, in Phase 13's uninstall sandbox which shares zero surface with this phase's change; the file passed **9/9 in isolation** (139 s) immediately afterwards, and a full-suite re-run on the identical tree came back **844 passed, 0 failed, 1 warning** (~560 s; warning = the pre-existing apprise/imghdr DeprecationWarning recorded since Phase 0; 809 prior + 35 new; DNS64-sensitive migration test passing). Frontend: `pnpm test` → **7 files / 40 passed**; `pnpm type-check` (tsc -b --noEmit) → exit 0; `pnpm exec oxlint src` → 0 errors / 12 warnings (= recorded baseline exactly). Backend `uv run --frozen ruff check .` → All checks passed!; `ruff format --check .` → 141 files formatted (both touched files formatted before the final runs). Directly-neighboring detection suites re-ran green mid-phase after the resolver fix (201 tests across phases 20/21/22/layers/floors/fusion-integration/e2e-flagging). No dependency changes → supply-chain gates stand green from Phase 9.
- **Interactions with prior fixes**: Phase 6 — gate membership untouched (layer 2 stays hash-gated; its input is the hashed string itself), gate suite green. Phase 7 — floor composition untouched and green (no l2 floor exists BY DESIGN; raising l2 fidelity cannot arm or bypass any floor). Phase 12 — e2e pins green unmodified (its fixture is inline-based; the fix strictly widens coverage). Phases 20–22 — sibling detection files untouched; their amended-fixture suites green inside the regression. Suppression seam verified unchanged and pinned. explain.py compatibility preserved by key-shape retention. Phases 8–10 — artifacts read-only; regeneration hand-off now covers layer-2 emissions (see residual). No earlier phase modified `worker/detection/dom.py` logic (verified via fix-log re-read + git log).
- **Residual risk / follow-ups**: (1) External stylesheet bytes are not captured anywhere in `PageData`; hiding that lives ONLY in `/styles.css` (linked, not embedded) remains invisible to this counter — closing it requires capture-contract changes (new artifact plumbing) and is recorded for Phase 42 to weigh knowingly. (2) Bare `@media screen` hiding and conditional-feature media queries are skipped wholesale by design (responsive/print FP elimination); a spammer hiding content inside such blocks evades this specific counter while remaining visible to churn/l8/l3. (3) Selector support is intentionally a subset: descendant context is not evaluated (documented over-match), specificity is approximated by document order (late generic resets could mask earlier class hides — FN-conservative), and CSS custom properties/var()-indirection is unresolved. (4) Framework class-toggling between captures can add legitimate new hidden elements (same tradeoff shape the product already accepts for inline toggles); delta semantics bound steady-state pages, and the operating point is recorded here for the post-cluster dataset/model regeneration to re-measure honestly. (5) Dataset/model regeneration hand-off STANDS and now includes layer 2: the builder's `hidden_spam_stealth` axis will measure HIGHER after this change; regenerate via the checked-in builder + trainer AFTER the emission-fix cluster completes (Phases 23–24?/36 — i.e., after Phase 36 per the workflow recorded in Phases 8/10/20/21/22), deliberately NOT regenerated here. (6) `docs/layers/2-dom-structure.mdx` accurately described the inline-only implementation and is now stale — docs-tree wording reserved for the scheduled Phase 34/41 sweeps per §4.0.5; the module docstring IS truthful as of this commit. (7) Session note: the transient pwsh crash in Phase 13's sandbox suite during the first full run passed in isolation and on full-suite re-run — environment/load-related, no action taken; if it recurs across sessions it may warrant a Phase 30 look at that suite's process-spawn robustness. (8) New leads observed: none.
- **Scratch hygiene**: probes lived in `%TEMP%\opencode\p23` (reverify×3, postfix_check, debug×2, failing_before runner), deleted after use; repo tree contains only intended changes plus the long-dispositioned items (`scripts/lib.ps1` user-intentional unstaged edit, untracked audit/recon files).
- **Commit**: fix(phase23) — technique-independent hidden-element detection: stylesheet-class resolution, non-display hiding markers, per-rule audit trail

### [FIXED] Capture/probe degradation is indistinguishable from "no change" in the fused feature vector — systematic failures silently disable layers while scans complete normally

- **Original severity**: Medium
- **Phase**: 24 — Detection Degradation-Signaling Gap
- **Files changed**: `backend/worker/detection/types.py` (new `degraded_result`; `skip_result` docstring narrowed to proof-of-zero semantics), `backend/worker/detection/pipeline.py` (crash isolation + baseline-artifact-missing branch now emit degraded results; docstring states the two skip kinds), `backend/worker/detection/visual.py`, `backend/worker/detection/cloaking.py`, `backend/worker/detection/metadata.py` (capture/probe-failure branches emit degraded results), `backend/worker/detection/fusion.py` (`_UNMEASURED_RISK_CEIL`, `_unmeasured_keys`, confidence-mass arithmetic in `layer9_fusion`), `backend/worker/scan_tasks.py` (`_summarize_layer_scores` helper carrying the degraded flag into `scans.layer_scores`), `backend/app/models.py` (layer_scores comment), `backend/app/schemas.py` (`SiteDetailOut.consecutive_degraded_scans`, `HealthDetails.sites_with_degraded_scans`), `backend/app/routers/sites.py` (`_consecutive_degraded_scans` walk on GET /api/sites/{id}), `backend/app/routers/health.py` (fleet aggregate on GET /api/health/details), `backend/tests/test_phase24_degradation_signaling.py` (new — 22 tests), plus three pre-existing test files updated where they codified the old defective shapes
- **Re-verification (Step 1)**: reproduced on current code with scratch probes outside the repo (deleted afterwards), driving the real pipeline/fusion. Identical DOM + identical real screenshots fused to 0.09270694…; identical DOM with BOTH screenshots missing fused to the byte-identical 0.09270694… with layer4 recorded `ran=True` (0.0-with-note); cloaking with zero UA variants scored like consistent-UAs; metadata with a fully-absent probe scored like a healthy probe; `build_feature_vector` returned numerically identical vectors for skipped vs measured-zero (only the never-consumed `ran` mask differed). Also re-measured post-Phase-6 relevance: with the visual gate gone, lost screenshot artifacts silently disabled the ONLY pixel ground truth while verdicts still read clean. Not stale; no prior phase had touched this mechanism.
- **Root cause (Step 2)**: two stacked gaps around one missing primitive — a third result state between "measured" and "structurally zero". (1) *Emission*: capture/probe failures never entered any "not run" state at all — visual/cloaking/metadata returned `layer_result(0.0, note)` ("I looked and saw nothing") for "I could not look". (2) *Fusion*: even genuine skips contributed feature 0.0 with the intercept applied at FULL strength regardless of how much evidence existed, so every dark channel read exactly as a trusted zero and pushed scores systematically toward benign. The hash gate's skips are genuinely different: byte-identical serialized DOM is a PROOF that the gated layers' values are zero — but the code represented proofs and absences identically.
- **Edge cases enumerated (Step 3)**:
  - *Concurrent access*: N/A — stateless pure functions per scan (same analysis as Phases 6/7/20–23); aggregates are bounded read queries.
  - *Empty/null/missing input*: result dict absent entirely ⇒ treated as unmeasured; blank screenshots/dead probes ⇒ degraded; robots.txt None on both sides deliberately NOT treated as degradation (404 is the normal steady state for most sites and compares stable-equal).
  - *Wrong-type/malformed*: malformed sub-scores keep their pinned coercion contract (feature 0.0, ran=True) — untouched this phase, documented residual.
  - *Unicode/boundary numerics*: confidence mass bounded (0,1] with a W_total<=0 guard falling back to ratio 1.0 (exact legacy math); uplift saturates at `_UNMEASURED_RISK_CEIL = 0.30`.
  - *Partial failure mid-operation*: partial degradation stays measured wherever ANY signal exists — e.g. a real robots change rescues an otherwise-dark metadata probe; one failed crawler variant beside a healthy reference keeps scoring (target-side refusal ≠ our capture breaking).
  - *Retry/idempotency*: deterministic pure functions; acks_late redelivery recomputes identical results.
  - *Auth/RBAC*: unchanged guards everywhere; the two new response fields sit behind existing CurrentUser surfaces.
  - *Interaction with other findings already fixed (Rule 11)*: Phase 6 — its five adversarial tests pass unmodified (relative assertions; real-PNG fixtures measure normally); its "missing screenshots report layer4" test rewritten to the degraded contract. Phase 7 floors — unchanged composition; degraded channels contribute 0.0 and cannot fire floors exactly as before; conclusive-signature floor re-proven binding beside dark channels. Phases 8–10 — artifact consumed read-only, coefficients/intercept untouched (binding guard test added); operating-point pins from Phase 12 survive BYTE-EXACT by construction (below).
